# #1073 — OmniRoute second-row dispositions, 2026-08-16 through 2026-08-31

Inclusive UTC merge window: **2026-08-16 through 2026-08-31**. Source: retained public metadata JSON in `/tmp/durindoor-1073-aug-eo1Czm`; no scratch audit scripts or candidate patch payloads were used. This record covers this date shard only.

## Inventory and path audit

The retained rows contain **1026** in-window PR records and **1026** unique PR IDs. All merge timestamps fall within the inclusive window. The returned changed-path list is complete for **939** rows; **87** rows retain fewer than `totalCount` paths and are explicitly unresolved with each omitted-path count shown. No missing paths were inferred. Every row includes the exact available source merge commit SHA and source PR link.

Disposition policy: only previous local evidence is treated as already present/ported; complete documentation, tests, CI or release-only path sets are upstream-only. Runtime and potentially qualifying dashboard changes remain unresolved unless reconciled by existing evidence. Provider onboarding/auth paths are grouped as Provider Sign-in. Titles and path names alone do not establish parity or a new gap.

## Reconciled focused August examples

- **#11921** is already present through local Ollama port #271; the prior August source-scope report records the streaming UTF-8 decoder evidence.
- **#11814** is already ported; the focused cache-write report records the local normalization and stream propagation evidence.
- **#11755** remains unresolved: the prior source-scope report confirms the `/v1/web/fetch` SSRF guard only and leaves other remote-image call sites unestablished.
- The earlier source-scope report’s examples were reconciled; this shard adds no newly confirmed port gap. No five-case deep review was used to convert ambiguous source changes into acceptance claims.

Refs #1073
